This Action stops the Action List on the current page and restarts processing on the next page.  After all pages have been processed like that, the Action List will resume on the current page with the Action after this one.  This makes it possible to go multiple times through a document within the same Action List, whereas normally an Action List is executed completely page after page and there is no way to move back to the beginning.
Use cases:
 Copy to a named clipboard from a later page and paste to earlier ones.
 Split pages first and change the order based on the split pages.
Keep in mind that selections are lost after this Action is executed, so it will be necessary to make a new selection.
